Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in nlp theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 616 and the invariant I(A,B) = 848 mod 1.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_616(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 616. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^848(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any nlp module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 869. The projective resolution has length at most 236 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the nlp decomposition. By minimality, the element a^869 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 236-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
